    59	function disableAutomaticFontScaling(Component) {
    60	  Component.defaultProps = Component.defaultProps || {};
    61	  Component.defaultProps.allowFontScaling = false;
    62	  Component.defaultProps.maxFontSizeMultiplier = 1;

    68	async function applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme({ dimStatusBar = false } = {}) {
    69	  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
    70	
    71	  try {
    72	    StatusBar.setHidden(false);
    73	    StatusBar.setTranslucent(false);
    74	    StatusBar.setBackgroundColor(
    75	      dimStatusBar ? androidStatusBarDimmedColor : androidNavigationBarColor,
    76	      true,
    77	    );
    78	    StatusBar.setBarStyle(androidStatusBarStyle, true);
    79	  } catch {}
    80	
    81	  try {
    82	    await NavigationBar.setVisibilityAsync("visible");
    83	  } catch {}
    84	
    85	  try {
    86	    await NavigationBar.setButtonStyleAsync(androidNavigationBarButtonStyle);
    87	  } catch {}
    88	}
    89	
    90	function AndroidNavigationBarTint({ dimStatusBar = false, pathname }) {
    91	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    92	
    93	  useEffect(() => {
    94	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") return undefined;
    95	
    96	    const applyTheme = () => applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme({ dimStatusBar });
    97	
    98	    applyTheme();
    99	    const restoreTimer = setTimeout(applyTheme, 250);
   100	    const finalRestoreTimer = setTimeout(applyTheme, 1000);
   101	    const appStateSubscription = AppState.addEventListener("change", (state) => {
   102	      if (state === "active") {
   103	        applyTheme();
   104	      }
   105	    });
   106	    let visibilitySubscription;
   107	
   108	    try {
   109	      visibilitySubscription = NavigationBar.addVisibilityListener(
   110	        applyTheme,
   111	      );
   112	    } catch {}
   113	
   114	    return () => {
   115	      clearTimeout(restoreTimer);
   116	      clearTimeout(finalRestoreTimer);
   117	      appStateSubscription.remove();
   118	      visibilitySubscription?.remove();
   119	    };
   120	  }, [dimStatusBar, pathname]);
   121	
   122	  if (Platform.OS !== "android" || insets.bottom <= 0) {
   123	    return null;
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  return (
   127	    <View
   128	      pointerEvents="none"
   129	      style={{
   130	        position: "absolute",
   131	        left: 0,
   132	        right: 0,
   133	        bottom: 0,
   134	        height: insets.bottom,
   135	        borderTopWidth: androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth,
   136	        borderTopColor: androidNavigationBarHairlineColor,
   137	        backgroundColor: androidNavigationBarColor,
   138	        zIndex: 1000001,
   139	        elevation: 1000001,
   140	      }}
   141	    />
   142	  );
   143	}
   144	
   145	function AndroidStatusBarTint({ dimmed = false }) {
   146	  const height =
   147	    Platform.OS === "android" && Number.isFinite(StatusBar.currentHeight)
   148	      ? Math.max(0, StatusBar.currentHeight)
   149	      : 0;
   150	
   151	  if (height <= 0) {
   152	    return null;
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  return (
   156	    <View
   157	      pointerEvents="none"
   158	      style={{
   159	        position: "absolute",
   160	        top: 0,
   161	        left: 0,
   162	        right: 0,
   163	        height,
   164	        backgroundColor: androidNavigationBarColor,
   165	        zIndex: 1000002,
   166	        elevation: 1000002,
   167	      }}
   168	    >
   169	      {dimmed ? (
   170	        <View
   171	          pointerEvents="none"
   172	          style={{
   173	            position: "absolute",
   174	            top: 0,
   175	            right: 0,
   176	            bottom: 0,
   177	            left: 0,
   178	            backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
   179	          }}
   180	        />
   181	      ) : null}
   182	    </View>
   183	  );
   184	}
   185	
   186	function RootLayoutContent({ headerScrollY }) {
   187	  const pathname = usePathname();
   188	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
   189	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   190	  const { cartOverlayActionRequest, isShopOverlayVisible } = useShopCart();
   191	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   192	  const topSafeInset = getTopSafeInset(insets);
   193	
   194	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
   195	
   196	  const useOverlayHeader =
   197	    pathname === "/" ||
   198	    pathname === "/products" ||
   199	    pathname === "/aboutus" ||
   200	    pathname === "/contact";
   201	
   202	  const shouldProtectBackgroundHero =
   203	    useOverlayHeader &&
   204	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive || backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen);
   205	  const backgroundHeroLayerDepth = shouldProtectBackgroundHero ? 100 : 0;
   206	  const screenFadeTopOffset = pathname === "/shop"
   207	    ? 120 + topSafeInset
   208	    : 84 + topSafeInset;
   209	  const shouldShowScreenFade =
   210	    !(pathname === "/shop" && isShopOverlayVisible);
   211	  const shouldShowCartOpeningDim =
   212	    cartOverlayActionRequest.pending && pathname !== "/shop";
   213	  const shouldDimAndroidStatusBar =
   214	    isSmallAndroidViewport && pathname === "/shop" && isShopOverlayVisible;
   215	
   216	  return (
   217	    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
   218	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   219	        <View
   220	          pointerEvents="none"
   221	          style={{
   222	            position: "absolute",
   223	            top: 0,
   224	            left: 0,
   225	            right: 0,
   226	            zIndex: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   227	            elevation: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   228	          }}
   229	        >
   230	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showOnlyHero />
   231	        </View>
   232	      ) : null}
   233	
   234	      <MainScreenPushFrame>
   235	        <Stack
   236	          screenOptions={{
   237	            headerShown: false,
   238	            animation: "none",
   239	            contentStyle: {
   240	              backgroundColor: useOverlayHeader
   241	                ? "transparent"
   242	                : "#FFFCF2",
   243	            },
   244	          }}
   245	        />
   246	      </MainScreenPushFrame>
   247	
   248	      {shouldShowScreenFade ? (
   249	        <ScreenFade
   250	          showTopFade={!useOverlayHeader}
   251	          topOffset={screenFadeTopOffset}
   252	        />
   253	      ) : null}
   254	
   255	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   256	        <View
   257	          pointerEvents="box-none"
   258	          style={{
   259	            position: "absolute",
   260	            top: 0,
   261	            left: 0,
   262	            right: 0,
   263	            zIndex: 1000000,
   264	            elevation: 1000000,
   265	          }}
   266	        >
   267	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showHero={false} />
   268	        </View>
   269	      ) : null}
   270	
   271	      {showPersistentHeader && !useOverlayHeader ? (
   272	        <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
   273	      ) : null}
   274	
   275	      {shouldShowCartOpeningDim ? (
   276	        <View
   277	          pointerEvents="none"
   278	          style={{
   279	            position: "absolute",
   280	            top: 0,
   281	            right: 0,
   282	            bottom: 0,
   283	            left: 0,
   284	            backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
   285	            zIndex: 1000001,
   286	            elevation: 1000001,
   287	          }}
   288	        />
   289	      ) : null}
   290	
   291	      <StickyCartButton />
   292	
   293	      <AndroidStatusBarTint
   294	        dimmed={shouldDimAndroidStatusBar}
   295	      />
   296	      <AndroidNavigationBarTint
   297	        dimStatusBar={shouldDimAndroidStatusBar}
   298	        pathname={pathname}
   299	      />
   300	    </View>
   301	  );
   302	}
   303	
   304	export default function RootLayout() {
   305	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   306	
   307	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
   308	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
   309	    "Great Vibes": require("../assets/fonts/great_vibes/GreatVibes-Regular.ttf"),
   310	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
   311	    "TT Fors Demibold": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_demibold.ttf"),
   312	    "TT Fors Light": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_light.ttf"),
   313	  });
   314	
   315	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
   316	    return null;
   317	  }
   318	
   319	  return (
   320	    <SafeAreaProvider>
   321	      <StripeProvider
   322	        merchantIdentifier={stripeMerchantIdentifier || undefined}
   323	        publishableKey={stripePublishableKey}
   324	        setReturnUrlSchemeOnAndroid
   325	        urlScheme={stripeUrlScheme}
   326	      >
   327	        <ThemeProvider value={navigationTheme}>
   328	          <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
   329	            <BackgroundHeroStateProvider sourceScrollY={headerScrollY}>
   330	              <HeaderSwipeProvider>
   331	                <ShopCartProvider>
   332	                  <RootLayoutContent headerScrollY={headerScrollY} />
   333	                </ShopCartProvider>
   334	              </HeaderSwipeProvider>
   335	            </BackgroundHeroStateProvider>
   336	          </HeaderScrollProvider>
   337	        </ThemeProvider>
   338	      </StripeProvider>
   339	    </SafeAreaProvider>
   340	  );
   341	}

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import {
     3	  Animated,
     4	  Image,
     5	  StyleSheet,
     6	  Text,
     7	  View,
     8	  useWindowDimensions,
     9	} from "react-native";

    13	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";

    15	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
    16	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    17	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    18	import {
    19	  getFeatureImageWidth,
    20	  scaleLineHeight,
    21	} from "../utils/responsiveLayout";
    22	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    23	
    24	const passionImageBlendScrollDistance = 720;
    25	const passionImageBlendFeatherHeight = 170;
    26	const passionImageBlendOverlayColor = "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.92)";
    27	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    28	const homeFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    29	const homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    30	
    31	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    32	  return (
    33	    currentLayout &&
    34	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    35	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    36	  );
    37	}
    38	
    39	export default function HomeScreen() {
    40	  const [imageLayouts, setImageLayouts] = useState({});
    41	  const [paragraphLayouts, setParagraphLayouts] = useState({});
    42	  const {
    43	    compactTopLayout,
    44	    initialContentOffset,
    45	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    46	    scrollHandlers,
    47	    scrollY,
    48	  } =
    49	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    50	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    51	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    52	  const croppedImageWidth = getFeatureImageWidth(windowWidth);
    53	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    54	  const passionImageBlendTranslateY = scrollY.interpolate({
    55	    inputRange: [0, passionImageBlendScrollDistance],
    56	    outputRange: [-passionImageBlendFeatherHeight, croppedImageHeight],
    57	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    58	  });
    59	  const handleParagraphLayout = useCallback(
    60	    (featureName) =>
    61	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    62	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    63	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    64	
    65	        setParagraphLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    66	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    67	            return currentLayouts;
    68	          }
    69	
    70	          return {
    71	            ...currentLayouts,
    72	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    73	          };
    74	        });
    75	      },
    76	    []
    77	  );
    78	  const handleImageLayout = useCallback(
    79	    (featureName) =>
    80	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    81	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    82	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    83	
    84	        setImageLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    85	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    86	            return currentLayouts;
    87	          }
    88	
    89	          return {
    90	            ...currentLayouts,
    91	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    92	          };
    93	        });
    94	      },
    95	    []
    96	  );
    97	
    98	  return (
    99	    <View style={sharedStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   100	      <Animated.ScrollView
   101	        style={sharedStyles.scroll}
   102	        contentContainerStyle={[
   103	          sharedStyles.scrollContent,
   104	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   105	        ]}
   106	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   107	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   108	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   109	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   110	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   111	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   112	        {...scrollHandlers}
   113	      >
   114	        <View style={sharedStyles.main}>
   115	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   116	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   117	            pageTitleStyle={[sharedStyles.pageTitle, homeStyles.pageTitle]}
   118	            scrollY={scrollY}
   119	          />
   120	
   121	          <CenterMagnifyView
   122	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.passion}
   123	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.passion}
   124	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   125	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   126	            scrollY={scrollY}
   127	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   128	          >
   129	            <View
   130	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("passion")}
   131	              style={[
   132	                sharedStyles.directionalImageBlendWrap,
   133	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
   134	              ]}
   135	            >
   136	              <Image
   137	                pointerEvents="none"
   138	                source={require("../passion111_mos9_bright_italian_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   139	                style={[
   140	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   141	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   142	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   143	                ]}
   144	                resizeMode="contain"
   145	              />
   146	              <Animated.View
   147	                pointerEvents="none"
   148	                style={[
   149	                  sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendPanel,
   150	                  {
   151	                    height:
   152	                      croppedImageHeight + passionImageBlendFeatherHeight,
   153	                    transform: [{ translateY: passionImageBlendTranslateY }],
   154	                  },
   155	                ]}
   156	              >
   157	                <LinearGradient
   158	                  colors={[
   159	                    "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
   160	                    passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   161	                  ]}
   162	                  locations={[0, 1]}
   163	                  style={sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendFeather}
   164	                />
   165	                <View
   166	                  style={[
   167	                    sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendSolid,
   168	                    {
   169	                      height: croppedImageHeight,
   170	                      backgroundColor: passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   171	                    },
   172	                  ]}
   173	                />
   174	              </Animated.View>
   175	            </View>
   176	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   177	              Passion
   178	            </Text>
   179	            <Text
   180	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("passion")}
   181	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   182	            >
   183	              Every Alla Vostra board is prepared with care, precision, and a
   184	              love for the grazing experience.
   185	            </Text>
   186	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   187	
   188	          <PageDivider />
   189	
   190	          <CenterMagnifyView
   191	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.taste}
   192	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.taste}
   193	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   194	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   195	            scrollY={scrollY}
   196	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   197	          >
   198	            <View
   199	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("taste")}
   200	              style={{ width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight }}
   201	            >
   202	              <Image
   203	                pointerEvents="none"
   204	                source={require("../taste111_mos9_bright_soft_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   205	                style={[
   206	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   207	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   208	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   209	                ]}
   210	                resizeMode="contain"
   211	              />
   212	            </View>
   213	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   214	              Taste
   215	            </Text>
   216	            <Text
   217	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("taste")}
   218	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   219	            >
   220	              Our boards are built around layered flavor: cheeses, meats,
   221	              fruits, sweets, spreads, and accoutrements.
   222	            </Text>
   223	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   224	
   225	          <PageDivider />
   226	
   227	          <CenterMagnifyView
   228	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.convenience}
   229	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.convenience}
   230	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   231	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   232	            scrollY={scrollY}
   233	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   234	          >

   242	                style={[
   243	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   244	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },

   110	
   111	function ProductSection({
   112	  croppedImageWidth,
   113	  onBuyPress,
   114	  onBuyButtonLayout,
   115	  onImageLayout,
   116	  onLowerContentLayout,
   117	  product,
   118	}) {
   119	  return (
   120	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
   121	      <View onLayout={onImageLayout} style={{ width: croppedImageWidth }}>
   122	        <View style={productsStyles.productImageWrap}>

   124	            pointerEvents="none"
   125	            source={product.image}
   126	            style={[
   127	              productsStyles.productImage,
   128	              { height: croppedImageWidth },
   129	            ]}
   130	            resizeMode="contain"
   131	            accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
   132	          />
   133	        </View>
   134	      </View>
   135	
   136	      <View
   137	        onLayout={onLowerContentLayout}
   138	        style={productsStyles.productLowerContent}
   139	      >
   140	        <Text style={productsStyles.productTitle}>{product.title}</Text>
   141	        <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   142	
   143	        <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   144	          {product.includes.map((section) => (
   145	            <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   146	              <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   147	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   148	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   149	              </View>
   150	
   151	              {section.items.map((item) => (
   152	                <View key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItemRow}>
   153	                  <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleBorder}>
   154	                    <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleOuter} />
   155	                    <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangle} />
   156	                  </View>
   157	                  <Text style={productsStyles.includeItem}>{item}</Text>
   158	                </View>
   159	              ))}
   160	
   161	              {section.note ? (
   162	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   163	              ) : null}
   164	            </View>
   165	          ))}
   166	        </View>
   167	
   168	        <Pressable
   169	          accessibilityLabel={`Buy ${product.title}`}
   170	          accessibilityRole="button"
   171	          onLayout={onBuyButtonLayout}
   172	          onPress={onBuyPress}
   173	          style={({ pressed }) => [
   174	            productsStyles.buyButton,
   175	            pressed && productsStyles.buyButtonPressed,
   176	          ]}
   177	        >
   178	          <ProductOrangeButtonGradient />
   179	          <Text
   180	            adjustsFontSizeToFit
   181	            allowFontScaling={false}
   182	            minimumFontScale={0.72}
   183	            numberOfLines={1}
   184	            style={productsStyles.buyButtonText}
   185	          >
   186	            {`Buy ${product.title}`}
   187	          </Text>
   188	        </Pressable>
   189	      </View>
   190	    </View>
   191	  );
   192	}
   193	
   194	function ProductMagnifySection({ croppedImageWidth, onBuyPress, product, scrollY }) {
   195	  const [buyButtonLayout, setBuyButtonLayout] = useState(null);
   196	  const [imageLayout, setImageLayout] = useState(null);
   197	  const [lowerContentLayout, setLowerContentLayout] = useState(null);
   198	
   199	  const updateLayout = useCallback((setter) => ({ nativeEvent }) => {
   200	    const nextLayout = nativeEvent.layout;
   201	
   202	    setter((currentLayout) => {
   203	      if (
   204	        currentLayout &&
   205	        Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
   206	        Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
   207	      ) {
   208	        return currentLayout;
   209	      }
   210	
   211	      return nextLayout;
   212	    });
   213	  }, []);
   214	
   215	  return (
   216	    <CenterMagnifyView
   217	      magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayout}
   218	      magnifyExitAnchorLayout={buyButtonLayout || lowerContentLayout}
   219	      scrollY={scrollY}
   220	      style={productsStyles.productSectionWrap}
   221	    >
   222	      <ProductSection
   223	        croppedImageWidth={croppedImageWidth}
   224	        onBuyButtonLayout={updateLayout(setBuyButtonLayout)}
   225	        onBuyPress={onBuyPress}
   226	        onImageLayout={updateLayout(setImageLayout)}
   227	        onLowerContentLayout={updateLayout(setLowerContentLayout)}
   228	        product={product}
   229	      />
   230	    </CenterMagnifyView>
   231	  );
   232	}
   233	
   234	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   235	  const {
   236	    compactTopLayout,
   237	    initialContentOffset,
   238	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
   239	    scrollHandlers,
   240	    scrollY,
   241	  } =
   242	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
   243	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
   244	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   245	  const croppedImageWidth = getFeatureImageWidth(windowWidth);
   246	  const openProductInShop = (productTitle) => {
   247	    router.push({
   248	      pathname: "/shop",
   249	      params: {
   250	        openProduct: String(Date.now()),
   251	        product: productTitle,
   252	      },
   253	    });
   254	  };
   255	
   256	  return (
   257	    <View style={productsStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   258	      <Animated.ScrollView
   259	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   260	        contentContainerStyle={[
   261	          productsStyles.scrollContent,
   262	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   263	        ]}
   264	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   265	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   266	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   267	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   268	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   269	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   270	        {...scrollHandlers}
   271	      >
   272	        <View style={productsStyles.main}>
   273	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   274	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   275	            pageTitleStyle={productsStyles.pageTitle}
   276	            scrollY={scrollY}
   277	          />
   278	
   279	          {products.map((product, index) => (
   280	            <Fragment key={product.title}>
   281	              <ProductMagnifySection
   282	                croppedImageWidth={croppedImageWidth}
   283	                onBuyPress={() => openProductInShop(product.title)}
   284	                product={product}
   285	                scrollY={scrollY}
   286	              />
   287	              {index < products.length - 1 ? <PageDivider /> : null}
   288	            </Fragment>
   289	          ))}
   290	        </View>
   291	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   292	    </View>
   293	  );
   294	}

FILE: mob/app/aboutus.js

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import { Animated, Image, Text, View, useWindowDimensions } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     5	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     6	import aboutusStyles from "../styles/aboutusStyles";
     7	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     8	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
     9	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    10	import { getFeatureImageWidth } from "../utils/responsiveLayout";
    11	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    12	
    13	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    14	const aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    15	const aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    16	
    17	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    18	  return (
    19	    currentLayout &&
    20	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    21	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    22	  );
    23	}
    24	
    25	export default function AboutusScreen() {
    26	  const [sectionLayouts, setSectionLayouts] = useState({});
    27	  const {
    28	    compactTopLayout,
    29	    initialContentOffset,
    30	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    31	    scrollHandlers,
    32	    scrollY,
    33	  } =
    34	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    35	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    36	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    37	  const croppedImageWidth = getFeatureImageWidth(windowWidth);
    38	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    39	  const handleSectionLayout = useCallback(
    40	    (sectionName) =>
    41	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    42	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    43	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    44	
    45	        setSectionLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    46	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[sectionName], nextLayout)) {
    47	            return currentLayouts;
    48	          }
    49	
    50	          return {
    51	            ...currentLayouts,
    52	            [sectionName]: nextLayout,
    53	          };
    54	        });
    55	      },
    56	    []
    57	  );
    58	
    59	  return (
    60	    <View style={aboutusStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    61	      <Animated.ScrollView
    62	        style={aboutusStyles.scroll}
    63	        contentContainerStyle={[
    64	          aboutusStyles.scrollContent,
    65	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
    66	        ]}
    67	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
    68	        decelerationRate={0.95}
    69	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
    70	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
    71	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
    72	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
    73	        {...scrollHandlers}
    74	      >
    75	        <View style={aboutusStyles.main}>
    76	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
    77	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
    78	            pageTitleStyle={aboutusStyles.pageTitle}
    79	            scrollY={scrollY}
    80	          />
    81	
    82	          <CenterMagnifyView
    83	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.image}
    84	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.image}
    85	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
    86	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}
    87	            scrollY={scrollY}
    88	            style={aboutusStyles.aboutSection}
    89	          >
    90	            <View
    91	              onLayout={handleSectionLayout("image")}
    92	              style={[
    93	                aboutusStyles.imageWrap,
    94	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
    95	              ]}
    96	            >
    97	              <Image
    98	                pointerEvents="none"
    99	                source={require("../convenience2_content_fill_mockup_1_plain_gray_gradient_mos9_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   100	                style={[
   101	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   102	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   103	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   104	                ]}
   105	                resizeMode="contain"
   106	              />
   107	            </View>
   108	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   109	
   110	          <CenterMagnifyView
   111	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph1}
   112	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph1}
   113	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   114	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   115	            scrollY={scrollY}
   116	            style={[aboutusStyles.aboutSection, aboutusStyles.copy]}
   117	          >
   118	            <Text
   119	              onLayout={handleSectionLayout("paragraph1")}
   120	              style={[
   121	                aboutusStyles.paragraph,
   122	                aboutusStyles.paragraphSectionGap,
   123	              ]}
   124	            >
   125	              Since we served up our very first cheeseplate, Alla Vostra has
   126	              been about one thing and one thing only: providing our customers
   127	              with the most intricate and delicious grazing boards you'll find
   128	              anywhere in the Miami metropolitan area.
   129	            </Text>

   139	          >

============================================================
FILE: mob/app/contact.js
============================================================

     1	import { useState } from "react";
     2	import { Alert, Animated, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     5	import HapticPressable from "../components/HapticPressable";
     6	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     7	import contactStyles from "../styles/contactStyles";
     8	import { sendContactMessage } from "../utils/contactMessages";
     9	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    10	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    11	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    12	
    13	export default function ContactScreen() {
    14	  const [name, setName] = useState("");
    15	  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    16	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
    17	  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
    18	  const [emptyTouchedFieldKeys, setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys] = useState({});
    19	  const [blurredFieldKeys, setBlurredFieldKeys] = useState({});
    20	  const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false);
    21	  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState("");
    22	  const {
    23	    compactTopLayout,
    24	    initialContentOffset,
    25	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    26	    scrollHandlers,
    27	    scrollY,
    28	  } =
    29	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    30	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    31	  const trimmedName = name.trim();
    32	  const trimmedEmail = email.trim();
    33	  const trimmedMessage = message.trim();
    34	  const isEmailValid = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(trimmedEmail);
    35	  const canSubmit =
    36	    trimmedName.length > 0 &&
    37	    isEmailValid &&
    38	    trimmedMessage.length > 0 &&
    39	    !isSending;
    40	
    41	  const contactFieldValues = {
    42	    email,
    43	    message,
    44	    name,
    45	    phone,
    46	  };
    47	  const shouldShowFieldFault = (fieldKey) =>
    48	    Boolean(emptyTouchedFieldKeys[fieldKey]) ||
    49	    (fieldKey === "email" &&
    50	      Boolean(blurredFieldKeys.email) &&
    51	      trimmedEmail.length > 0 &&
    52	      !isEmailValid);
    53	
    54	  const clearFieldFault = (fieldKey) => {
    55	    setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    56	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    57	        return currentFieldKeys;
    58	      }
    59	
    60	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    61	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    62	      return nextFieldKeys;
    63	    });
    64	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    65	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    66	        return currentFieldKeys;
    67	      }
    68	
    69	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    70	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    71	      return nextFieldKeys;
    72	    });
    73	  };
    74	
    75	  const markFieldBlurred = (fieldKey) => {
    76	    const fieldValue = contactFieldValues[fieldKey] || "";
    77	
    78	    setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
    79	      ...currentFieldKeys,
    80	      [fieldKey]: true,
    81	    }));
    82	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    83	      const isFieldEmpty = fieldValue.trim().length === 0;
    84	
    85	      if (isFieldEmpty) {
    86	        return {
    87	          ...currentFieldKeys,
    88	          [fieldKey]: true,
    89	        };
    90	      }
    91	
    92	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    93	        return currentFieldKeys;
    94	      }
    95	
    96	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    97	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    98	      return nextFieldKeys;
    99	    });
   100	  };
   101	
   102	  const updateContactField = (fieldKey, text, setter) => {
   103	    setter(text);
   104	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
   105	      if (text.trim().length === 0 || !currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
   106	        return currentFieldKeys;
   107	      }
   108	
   109	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
   110	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
   111	      return nextFieldKeys;
   112	    });
   113	  };
   114	
   115	  const showContactAlert = (title, alertMessage) => {
   116	    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.alert === "function") {
   117	      window.alert(`${title}\n${alertMessage}`);
   118	      return;
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    Alert.alert(title, alertMessage);
   122	  };
   123	
   124	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
   125	    if (!canSubmit) {
   126	      setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
   127	        ...currentFieldKeys,
   128	        email: true,
   129	      }));
   130	      setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
   131	        ...currentFieldKeys,
   132	        ...(trimmedName.length === 0 ? { name: true } : {}),
   133	        ...(trimmedEmail.length === 0 ? { email: true } : {}),
   134	        ...(trimmedMessage.length === 0 ? { message: true } : {}),
   135	      }));
   136	      setStatusMessage("Please enter your name, email, and message.");
   137	      return;
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    setIsSending(true);
   141	    setStatusMessage("");
   142	
   143	    try {
   144	      await sendContactMessage({
   145	        email: trimmedEmail,
   146	        message: trimmedMessage,
   147	        name: trimmedName,
   148	        phone: phone.trim(),
   149	      });
   150	      setName("");
   151	      setEmail("");
   152	      setPhone("");
   153	      setMessage("");
   154	      setBlurredFieldKeys({});
   155	      setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys({});
   156	      setStatusMessage("Message sent. We will respond within 48 hours.");
   157	      showContactAlert(
   158	        "Message sent",
   159	        "Thank you. Your message was sent to Alla Vostra.",
   160	      );
   161	    } catch (error) {
   162	      const errorMessage = error?.message || "Please try again.";
   163	      setStatusMessage(errorMessage);
   164	      showContactAlert("Message not sent", errorMessage);
   165	    } finally {
   166	      setIsSending(false);
   167	    }
   168	  };
   169	
   170	  return (
   171	    <View style={contactStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   172	      <Animated.ScrollView
   173	        style={contactStyles.scroll}
   174	        contentContainerStyle={[
   175	          contactStyles.scrollContent,
   176	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   177	        ]}
   178	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   179	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   180	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   181	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   182	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   183	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   184	        {...scrollHandlers}
   185	      >
   186	        <View style={contactStyles.main}>
   187	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   188	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   189	            pageTitleStyle={contactStyles.pageTitle}
   190	            scrollY={scrollY}
   191	          />
   192	
   193	          <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   194	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>Got a business inquiry?</Text>
   195	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>
   196	              General questions about our products?
   197	            </Text>
   198	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>
   199	              We strive to respond within 48 hours.
   200	            </Text>
   201	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   202	
   203	          <View style={contactStyles.formCard}>
   204	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   205	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Full Name</Text>
   206	              <TextInput
   207	                style={[
   208	                  contactStyles.input,
   209	                  shouldShowFieldFault("name") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   210	                ]}
   211	                placeholder="Your Name"
   212	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   213	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("name")}
   214	                onChangeText={(text) => updateContactField("name", text, setName)}
   215	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("name")}
   216	                returnKeyType="next"
   217	                textContentType="name"
   218	                value={name}
   219	              />
   220	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   221	
   222	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   223	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Email Address</Text>
   224	              <TextInput
   225	                style={[
   226	                  contactStyles.input,
   227	                  shouldShowFieldFault("email") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   228	                ]}
   229	                placeholder="you@company.com"
   230	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   231	                keyboardType="email-address"
   232	                autoCapitalize="none"
   233	                autoCorrect={false}
   234	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("email")}
   235	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   236	                  updateContactField("email", text, setEmail)
   237	                }
   238	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("email")}
   239	                returnKeyType="next"
   240	                textContentType="emailAddress"
   241	                value={email}
   242	              />
   243	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   244	
   245	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   246	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Phone Number</Text>
   247	              <TextInput
   248	                style={[
   249	                  contactStyles.input,
   250	                  shouldShowFieldFault("phone") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   251	                ]}
   252	                placeholder="+1 (555) 1234-567"
   253	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   254	                keyboardType="phone-pad"
   255	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("phone")}
   256	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   257	                  updateContactField("phone", text, setPhone)
   258	                }
   259	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("phone")}
   260	                returnKeyType="next"
   261	                textContentType="telephoneNumber"
   262	                value={phone}
   263	              />
   264	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   265	
   266	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   267	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Your Message</Text>
   268	              <TextInput
   269	                style={[
   270	                  contactStyles.input,
   271	                  contactStyles.messageInput,
   272	                  shouldShowFieldFault("message") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   273	                ]}
   274	                placeholder="Your Message"
   275	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   276	                multiline
   277	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("message")}
   278	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   279	                  updateContactField("message", text, setMessage)
   280	                }
   281	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("message")}
   282	                returnKeyType="default"
   283	                textAlignVertical="top"
   284	                value={message}
   285	              />
   286	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   287	
   288	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY} style={contactStyles.buttonWrap}>
   289	              <HapticPressable
   290	                accessibilityRole="button"
   291	                disabled={isSending}
   292	                onPress={handleSubmit}
   293	                style={({ pressed }) => [
   294	                  contactStyles.button,
   295	                  (!canSubmit || pressed) && contactStyles.buttonDimmed,
   296	                ]}
   297	              >
   298	                <Text style={contactStyles.buttonText}>
   299	                  {isSending ? "Sending..." : "Send Message"}
   300	                </Text>
   301	              </HapticPressable>
   302	              {statusMessage ? (
   303	                <Text style={contactStyles.statusText}>{statusMessage}</Text>
   304	              ) : null}
   305	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   306	          </View>
   307	        </View>
   308	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   309	    </View>
   310	  );
   311	}

   596	    case 2:

   600	    default:

   602	  }

   665	function getStripeCardBrandLabel(brand) {
   666	  switch (brand) {
   667	    case "Visa":
   668	      return "VISA";
   669	    case "MasterCard":
   670	      return "MASTERCARD";
   671	    case "AmericanExpress":
   672	      return "AMEX";
   673	    case "Unknown":
   674	    case undefined:
   675	    case null:
   676	      return "";
   677	    default:
   678	      return String(brand)
   679	        .replace(/([a-z])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2")
   680	        .toUpperCase();
   681	  }
   682	}

   878	function PiccolaQuantityTriangle({ direction, muted }) {
   879	  const strokeInset = piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth / 2;
   880	  const strokeColor = muted ? "#888888" : "#111111";
   881	  const isUpTriangle = direction === "up";
   882	  const fillGradientId = isUpTriangle
   883	    ? "piccolaQuantityTriangleTopGradient"
   884	    : "piccolaQuantityTriangleBottomGradient";
   885	  const fillGradientColors = isUpTriangle
   886	    ? topOverlayGradientColors
   887	    : orangeButtonGradientColors;
   888	  const path =
   889	    isUpTriangle
   890	      ? [
   891	          `M ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${strokeInset}`,
   892	          `L ${
   893	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   894	          } ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset}`,
   895	          `L ${strokeInset} ${
   896	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   897	          }`,
   898	          "Z",
   899	        ].join(" ")
   900	      : [
   901	          `M ${strokeInset} ${strokeInset}`,
   902	          `L ${
   903	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   904	          } ${strokeInset}`,
   905	          `L ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${
   906	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   907	          }`,
   908	          "Z",
   909	        ].join(" ");
   910	
   911	  return (
   912	    <Svg
   913	      height="100%"
   914	      style={shopStyles.piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleSvg}
   915	      viewBox={`0 0 ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth} ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}`}
   916	      width="100%"
   917	    >
   918	      <Defs>
   919	        <SvgLinearGradient
   920	          gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"
   921	          id={fillGradientId}
   922	          x1={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2}
   923	          x2={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2}
   924	          y1={isUpTriangle ? piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight : 0}
   925	          y2={isUpTriangle ? 0 : piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}
   926	        >
   927	          {fillGradientColors.map((color, index) => (
   928	            <Stop
   929	              key={`${fillGradientId}-${color}`}
   930	              offset={`${index * 50}%`}
   931	              stopColor={color}
   932	            />
   933	          ))}
   934	        </SvgLinearGradient>
   935	      </Defs>
   936	      <Path
   937	        d={path}
   938	        fill={`url(#${fillGradientId})`}
   939	        opacity={muted ? 0.5 : 1}
   940	        stroke={strokeColor}
   941	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   942	        strokeWidth={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth}
   943	      />
   944	    </Svg>
   945	  );
   946	}

  1718	      }
  1719	    });
  1720	  };
  1721	
  1722	  const selectDeliveryStateOption = (option) => {
  1723	    setSelectedDeliveryState(option);
  1724	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(null);
  1725	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1726	
  1727	    if (option !== "FL") {
  1728	      shakeDeliveryOverlay();
  1729	    }
  1730	  };
  1731	
  1732	  const selectDeliveryCityOption = (option) => {
  1733	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1734	      ...currentValues,
  1735	      city: option,
  1736	    }));
  1737	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(null);
  1738	    setIsDeliveryCityDropdownOpen(false);
  1739	
  1740	    if (!isDeliveryServiceAreaCity(option)) {
  1741	      shakeDeliveryOverlay();
  1742	    }
  1743	  };
  1744	
  1745	  const updateDeliveryFieldValue = (fieldKey, text) => {
  1746	    const nextValue = fieldKey === "zip" ? normalizeDeliveryZip(text) : text;
  1747	
  1748	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1749	      ...currentValues,
  1750	      [fieldKey]: nextValue,
  1751	    }));
  1752	    setEmptyTouchedDeliveryFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
  1753	      if (nextValue.trim().length === 0 || !currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
  1754	        return currentFieldKeys;
  1755	      }
  1756	
  1757	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
  1758	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
  1759	      return nextFieldKeys;
  1760	    });
  1761	
  1762	    if (
  1763	      fieldKey === "zip" &&
  1764	      nextValue.length === 5 &&
  1765	      !isDeliveryServiceAreaZip(nextValue)
  1766	    ) {
  1767	      shakeDeliveryOverlay();
  1768	    }
  1769	  };
  1770	
  1771	  const toggleDeliveryTimeDropdown = (fieldKey) => {
  1772	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(fieldKey);
  1773	    setIsDeliveryCityDropdownOpen(false);
  1774	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1775	    setIsPaymentIssuerDropdownOpen(false);
  1776	
  1777	    if (openDeliveryTimeDropdownKey !== fieldKey) {
  1778	      measureDeliveryTimeDropdownAnchor(fieldKey);
  1779	      setOpenDeliveryTimeDropdownKey(fieldKey);
  1780	      return;
  1781	    }
  1782	
  1783	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(null);
  1784	    setOpenDeliveryTimeDropdownKey(null);
  1785	  };
  1786	
  1787	  const selectDeliveryTimeDropdownOption = (fieldKey, option) => {
  1788	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1789	      ...currentValues,
  1790	      [fieldKey]: option,
  1791	    }));
  1792	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(null);
  1793	    setIsDeliveryCityDropdownOpen(false);
  1794	    setOpenDeliveryTimeDropdownKey(null);
  1795	  };
  1796	
  1797	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex = (options, optionIndex) =>
  1798	    deliveryTimeWheelLoopMidpoint * options.length + optionIndex;
  1799	
  1800	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY = (scrollY) =>
  1801	    Math.max(0, Math.round(scrollY / deliveryTimeWheelScrollStepHeight));
  1802	
  1803	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelOptionIndexFromScrollY = (options, scrollY) => {
  1804	    if (options.length === 0) {
  1805	      return 0;
  1806	    }
  1807	
  1808	    const loopedIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY(scrollY);
  1809	
  1810	    return ((loopedIndex % options.length) + options.length) % options.length;
  1811	  };
  1812	
  1813	  const setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex = (fieldKey, loopedIndex) => {
  1814	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleIndexes((currentIndexes) => {
  1815	      if (currentIndexes[fieldKey] === loopedIndex) {
  1816	        return currentIndexes;
  1817	      }
  1818	
  1819	      return {
  1820	        ...currentIndexes,
  1821	        [fieldKey]: loopedIndex,
  1822	      };
  1823	    });
  1824	  };
  1825	
  1826	  const triggerDeliveryTimeWheelTick = (fieldKey, loopedIndex) => {
  1827	    if (
  1828	      Platform.OS !== "android" ||
  1829	      !deliveryTimeWheelIsDraggingRef.current[fieldKey] ||
  1830	      deliveryTimeWheelHapticIndexesRef.current[fieldKey] === loopedIndex
  1831	    ) {
  1832	      return;
  1833	    }
  1834	
  1835	    deliveryTimeWheelHapticIndexesRef.current[fieldKey] = loopedIndex;
  1836	    triggerShopInteractionTick();
  1837	  };
  1838	
  1839	  const updateDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleIndex = (fieldKey, scrollY) => {
  1840	    const loopedIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY(scrollY);
  1841	
  1842	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex(fieldKey, loopedIndex);
  1843	    triggerDeliveryTimeWheelTick(fieldKey, loopedIndex);
  1844	  };
  1845	
  1846	  const scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex = (
  1847	    fieldKey,
  1848	    optionIndex,
  1849	    options,
  1850	    animated = true,
  1851	  ) => {
  1852	    const offset =
  1853	      getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex(options, optionIndex) *
  1854	      deliveryTimeWheelScrollStepHeight;
  1855	
  1856	    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  1857	      const scrollNode = deliveryTimeWheelScrollRefs.current[fieldKey];
  1858	
  1859	      if (scrollNode?.scrollToOffset) {
  1860	        scrollNode.scrollToOffset({ animated, offset });
  1861	        return;
  1862	      }
  1863	
  1864	      scrollNode?.scrollTo?.({
  1865	        animated,
  1866	        y: offset,
  1867	      });
  1868	    });
  1869	  };
  1870	
  1871	  const setDeliveryTimeWheelValue = (
  1872	    fieldKey,
  1873	    option,
  1874	    options,
  1875	    shouldScroll = true,
  1876	  ) => {
  1877	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1878	      ...currentValues,
  1879	      [fieldKey]: option,
  1880	    }));
  1881	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(fieldKey);
  1882	    setIsDeliveryCityDropdownOpen(false);
  1883	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1884	    setOpenDeliveryTimeDropdownKey(null);
  1885	    setIsPaymentIssuerDropdownOpen(false);
  1886	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex(
  1887	      fieldKey,
  1888	      getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex(
  1889	        options,
  1890	        Math.max(0, options.indexOf(option)),
  1891	      ),
  1892	    );
  1893	
  1894	    if (shouldScroll) {
  1895	      scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex(
  1896	        fieldKey,
  1897	        Math.max(0, options.indexOf(option)),
  1898	        options,
  1899	        true,
  1900	      );
  1901	    }
  1902	  };
  1903	
  1904	  const settleDeliveryTimeWheel = (fieldKey, options, scrollY) => {
  1905	    const optionIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelOptionIndexFromScrollY(
  1906	      options,
  1907	      scrollY,
  1908	    );
  1909	
  1910	    setDeliveryTimeWheelValue(fieldKey, options[optionIndex], options, false);
  1911	    deliveryTimeWheelIsDraggingRef.current[fieldKey] = false;
  1912	    scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex(fieldKey, optionIndex, options, false);
  1913	  };
  1914	
  1915	  const togglePaymentIssuerDropdown = () => {

  1980	  const selectPaymentWalletMethod = async (method) => {
  1981	    setSelectedPaymentOverlayMethod(method);
  1982	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(null);
  1983	    setIsPaymentCardDetailsOverlayVisible(false);
  1984	    setStripeCardDetails(null);
  1985	    stripeCardDetailsRef.current = null;
  1986	    setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(null);
  1987	    setIsPaymentCardAccepted(false);
  1988	
  1989	    if (method === paymentOverlayPayPalMethod) {
  1990	      setIsPaymentOrderConfirmationVisible(false);
  1991	      setIsOrderPlacementConfirmed(false);
  1992	      setIsPaymentPayPalOverlayVisible(true);
  1993	      return;
  1994	    }
  1995	
  1996	    setIsPaymentPayPalOverlayVisible(false);
  1997	
  1998	    if (
  1999	      method !== paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod &&
  2000	      method !== paymentOverlayApplePayMethod
  2001	    ) {
  2002	      return;
  2003	    }
  2004	
  2005	    if (method === paymentOverlayApplePayMethod) {
  2006	      if (Platform.OS !== "ios") {
  2007	        showPaymentAlert(
  2008	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  2009	          "Apple Pay is only available on iPhone. Use Debit/Credit Card or Google Pay on this device.",
  2010	        );
  2011	        return;
  2012	      }
  2013	
  2014	      if (isExpoGo) {
  2015	        showPaymentAlert(
  2016	          "Apple Pay needs a development build",
  2017	          "Expo Go cannot open the native Apple Pay sheet. Use an iOS development build or App Store build to test Apple Pay.",
  2018	        );
  2019	        return;
  2020	      }
  2021	
  2022	      if (!stripeMerchantIdentifier) {
  2023	        showPaymentAlert(
  2024	          "Apple Pay setup needed",
  2025	          "Add EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_MERCHANT_IDENTIFIER to mob/.env so StripeProvider can use your Apple merchant ID.",
  2026	        );
  2027	        return;
  2028	      }
  2029	
  2030	      if (!isApplePaySupported) {
  2031	        showPaymentAlert(
  2032	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  2033	          "This iPhone is not ready for Apple Pay. Make sure Wallet has an active card, then try again.",
  2034	        );
  2035	      }
  2036	
  2037	      return;
  2038	    }
  2039	
  2040	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  2041	      showPaymentAlert(
  2042	        "Google Pay unavailable",
  2043	        "Google Pay is only available on Android. Use Debit/Credit Card on this device.",
  2044	      );
  2045	      return;
  2046	    }
  2047	
  2048	    if (isExpoGo) {
  2049	      showPaymentAlert(
  2050	        "Google Pay needs a development build",
  2051	        "Expo Go cannot open the native Google Pay sheet. Use an Android development build or Play Store build to test Google Pay.",
  2052	      );
  2053	      return;
  2054	    }
  2055	
  2056	    if (!isGooglePaySupported) {
  2057	      showPaymentAlert(
  2058	        "Google Pay unavailable",
  2059	        "This device is not ready for Google Pay. Make sure Google Wallet is set up, then try again.",
  2060	      );
  2061	    }
  2062	  };
  2063	
  2064	  const acceptPaymentCardDetailsOverlay = async () => {
  2065	    const latestCardDetails = stripeCardDetailsRef.current || stripeCardDetails;
  2066	
  2067	    if (!latestCardDetails?.complete) {
  2068	      setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(null);
  2069	      setIsPaymentCardAccepted(false);
  2070	      showPaymentAlert(
  2071	        "Card details needed",
  2072	        "Enter a complete card number, expiration date, and CVV.",
  2073	      );
  2074	      return;
  2075	    }
  2076	
  2077	    const paymentMethodResult = await createPaymentMethod({
  2078	      paymentMethodType: "Card",
  2079	      paymentMethodData: {
  2080	        billingDetails: buildStripeBillingDetails(),
  2081	      },
  2082	    });
  2083	
  2084	    if (paymentMethodResult.error || !paymentMethodResult.paymentMethod?.id) {
  2085	      setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(null);
  2086	      setIsPaymentCardAccepted(false);
  2087	      showPaymentAlert(
  2088	        "Card details needed",
  2089	        paymentMethodResult.error?.localizedMessage ||
  2090	          paymentMethodResult.error?.message ||
  2091	          "Stripe could not save those card details. Please check the card number, expiration date, and CVV.",
  2092	      );
  2093	      return;
  2094	    }
  2095	
  2096	    setStripeCardDetails(latestCardDetails);
  2097	    setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(paymentMethodResult.paymentMethod.id);
  2098	    setSelectedPaymentOverlayMethod(paymentOverlayCardMethod);
  2099	    setIsPaymentCardAccepted(true);
  2100	    setIsPaymentCardDetailsOverlayVisible(false);
  2101	  };

  2103	  const showPaymentAlert = (title, message) => {

  2105	      window.alert(`${title}\n${message}`);

  2112	  const buildStripeOrderPayload = () => ({

  2275	  const clearOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger = () => {

  2280	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2281	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(0);

  2284	  const startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
  2285	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();

  2287	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2288	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2289	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
  2290	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
  2291	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
  2292	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0,
  2293	    );
  2294	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
  2295	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0,
  2296	    );
  2297	  };
  2298	
  2299	  const showOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
  2300	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2301	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
  2302	  };
  2303	
  2304	  const hideOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
  2305	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2306	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2307	  };
  2308	
  2309	  const overlayVisibleArrowOpacity = overlayHeldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
  2310	    inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
  2311	    outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
  2312	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2313	  });
  2314	  const overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
  2315	    overlayVisibleArrowOpacity,
  2316	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
  2317	      inputRange: [0, 1],
  2318	      outputRange: [1, 0],
  2319	      extrapolate: "clamp",
  2320	    }),
  2321	  );
  2322	  const overlayLeftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
  2323	    overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity,
  2324	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity,
  2325	  ).interpolate({
  2326	    inputRange: [0, 1],

  2329	  });

  2337	  });

  2339	    overlayImageStageWidth / 2 + piccolaOverlayImageHalfSize,
  2340	    150,
  2341	  );

  2343	    overlayImageDirection < 0
  2344	      ? overlayImageTravelDistance
  2345	      : -overlayImageTravelDistance;

  2349	    outputRange: [overlayIncomingStartOffset, 0],

  2354	    outputRange: [0, overlayOutgoingEndOffset],
  2355	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2356	  });
  2357	  const overlayIncomingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2358	    inputRange: [0, 0.52, 0.82, 1],
  2359	    outputRange: [0, 0.08, 0.68, 1],
  2360	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2361	  });
  2362	  const overlayOutgoingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2363	    inputRange: [0, 0.18, 0.36, 1],
  2364	    outputRange: [1, 0.22, 0, 0],
  2365	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2366	  });

  2368	  const getOverlayProductTransitionDirection = (fromName, toName) => {
  2369	    const fromIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2370	      (product) => product.name === fromName,
  2371	    );
  2372	    const toIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2373	      (product) => product.name === toName,
  2374	    );
  2375	
  2376	    if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) {
  2377	      return -1;
  2378	    }
  2379	
  2380	    const lastOverlayIndex = overlayNavProducts.length - 1;
  2381	
  2382	    if (fromIndex === lastOverlayIndex && toIndex === 0) return -1;
  2383	    if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastOverlayIndex) return 1;
  2384	
  2385	    return toIndex > fromIndex ? -1 : 1;
  2386	  };
  2387	
  2388	  const transitionOverlayProductImage = (nextProductName, direction) => {
  2389	    if (!nextProductName || nextProductName === activeOverlayProductName) {
  2390	      return;
  2391	    }
  2392	
  2393	    if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
  2394	      const previousAnimation = overlayImageAnimationRef.current;
  2395	      overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  2396	      previousAnimation.stop();
  2397	    }
  2398	
  2399	    if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
  2400	      const previousNavAnimation = overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current;
  2401	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  2402	      previousNavAnimation.stop();
  2403	    }
  2404	
  2405	    const nextOverlayNavIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2406	      (product) => product.name === nextProductName,
  2407	    );
  2408	
  2409	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
  2410	    setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(activeOverlayProductName);
  2411	    setOverlayImageDirection(direction);
  2412	    overlayImageProgress.setValue(0);
  2413	    setActiveOverlayProductName(nextProductName);
  2414	
  2415	    if (nextOverlayNavIndex >= 0) {
  2416	      const navAnimation = Animated.timing(overlayNavIndicatorProgress, {
  2417	        toValue: nextOverlayNavIndex,
  2418	        duration: 320,
  2419	        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  2420	        useNativeDriver: true,
  2421	      });
  2422	
  2423	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = navAnimation;
  2424	      navAnimation.start(() => {
  2425	        if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current === navAnimation) {
  2426	          overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  2427	        }
  2428	      });
  2429	    }
  2430	
  2431	    const animation = Animated.timing(overlayImageProgress, {
  2432	      toValue: 1,
  2433	      duration: 320,
  2434	      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  2435	      useNativeDriver: true,
  2436	    });
  2437	
  2438	    overlayImageAnimationRef.current = animation;
  2439	
  2440	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
  2441	      if (finished) {
  2442	        setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(null);
  2443	      }
  2444	
  2445	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current === animation) {
  2446	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  2447	      }
  2448	    });
  2449	  };

  2451	  const handleOverlayProductNameSelect = (nextProductName) => {

  2472	  const goToOverlayPreviousProduct = () => {

  2496	  const getOverlayBandTouchPoint = (event) => {
  2497	    const primaryTouch =
  2498	      event?.nativeEvent?.touches?.[0] ||
  2499	      event?.nativeEvent?.changedTouches?.[0] ||
  2500	      event?.nativeEvent;
  2501	
  2502	    return {
  2503	      x:
  2504	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageX === "number"
  2505	          ? primaryTouch.pageX
  2506	          : primaryTouch?.locationX,
  2507	      y:
  2508	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageY === "number"
  2509	          ? primaryTouch.pageY
  2510	          : primaryTouch?.locationY,
  2511	    };
  2512	  };
  2513	
  2514	  const handleOverlayBandTouchStart = (event) => {
  2515	    const { x, y } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
  2516	
  2517	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = x;
  2518	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = y;
  2519	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
  2520	    showOverlayHeldArrows();
  2521	  };
  2522	
  2523	  const handleOverlayBandTouchMove = (event) => {
  2524	    if (overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current) return;
  2525	
  2526	    const startX = overlaySwipeStartXRef.current;
  2527	    const startY = overlaySwipeStartYRef.current;
  2528	    const { x: currentX, y: currentY } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
  2529	
  2530	    if (
  2531	      typeof startX !== "number" ||
  2532	      typeof startY !== "number" ||
  2533	      typeof currentX !== "number" ||
  2534	      typeof currentY !== "number"
  2535	    ) {
  2536	      return;
  2537	    }
  2538	
  2539	    const dx = currentX - startX;
  2540	    const dy = Math.abs(currentY - startY);
  2541	    const minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance = 12.5;
  2542	    const horizontalDominanceRatio = 0.4375;
  2543	    const isHorizontalSwipeIntent =
  2544	      Math.abs(dx) >= minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance &&
  2545	      Math.abs(dx) > dy * horizontalDominanceRatio;
  2546	
  2547	    if (!isHorizontalSwipeIntent) return;
  2548	
  2549	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = true;
  2550	
  2551	    if (dx < 0) {
  2552	      goToOverlayNextProduct();
  2553	    } else {
  2554	      goToOverlayPreviousProduct();
  2555	    }
  2556	  };
  2557	
  2558	  const handleOverlayBandTouchEnd = () => {
  2559	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = null;
  2560	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = null;
  2561	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
  2562	    hideOverlayHeldArrows();
  2563	  };
  2564

  2571	      clearOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger();
  2572	      overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current = null;
  2573	    }, 130);
  2574	
  2575	    return () => {
  2576	      if (overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current) {
  2577	        clearTimeout(overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current);
  2578	      }
  2579	    };
  2580	  }, [activeOverlayProductName]);

  2588	      if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {

  3610	  const resetShopCheckoutFlow = () => {

  3618	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;

  3620	
  3621	    if (shippingPreviewActionBandAnimationRef.current) {

  3624	    }

  3628	      deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current = null;
  3629	    }
  3630	
  3631	    products.forEach((product) => {

  3634	      discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(product.name);

  3641	    setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(null);

  3718	  const showOrderPlacementConfirmation = () => {

  3720	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(false);
  3721	    setIsContactOverlayVisible(false);
  3722	    setIsTimeOverlayVisible(false);
  3723	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  3724	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  3725	    setIsPaymentCardDetailsOverlayVisible(false);
  3726	    setIsPaymentPayPalOverlayVisible(false);
  3727	    setIsPaymentOrderConfirmationVisible(false);
  3728	    setIsPlaceholderOverlayVisible(true);
  3729	    setIsOrderPlacementConfirmed(true);
  3730	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  3731	  };
  3732	  const handleOrderConfirmationYesPress = async () => {
  3733	    if (isStripePaymentInFlight) return;
  3734	
  3735	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
  3736	      showPaymentAlert(
  3737	        "Payment unavailable",
  3738	        "Stripe payments are available in the mobile app build.",
  3739	      );
  3740	      return;
  3741	    }
  3742	
  3743	    const configurationIssue = getStripeConfigurationIssue();
  3744	
  3745	    if (configurationIssue) {
  3746	      showPaymentAlert("Stripe setup needed", configurationIssue);
  3747	      return;
  3748	    }
  3749	
  3750	    if (
  3751	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayCardMethod &&
  3752	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod &&
  3753	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayApplePayMethod
  3754	    ) {
  3755	      showPaymentAlert(
  3756	        "Payment method unavailable",
  3757	        "Use Debit/Credit Card, Google Pay, or Apple Pay for this Stripe checkout.",
  3758	      );
  3759	      return;
  3760	    }
  3761	
  3762	    if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayApplePayMethod) {
  3763	      if (Platform.OS !== "ios") {
  3764	        showPaymentAlert(
  3765	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  3766	          "Apple Pay is only available on iPhone. Use Debit/Credit Card or Google Pay on this device.",
  3767	        );
  3768	        return;
  3769	      }
  3770	
  3771	      if (isExpoGo) {
  3772	        showPaymentAlert(
  3773	          "Apple Pay needs a development build",
  3774	          "Expo Go cannot open the native Apple Pay sheet. Use an iOS development build or App Store build to test Apple Pay.",
  3775	        );
  3776	        return;
  3777	      }
  3778	
  3779	      if (!stripeMerchantIdentifier) {
  3780	        showPaymentAlert(
  3781	          "Apple Pay setup needed",
  3782	          "Add EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_MERCHANT_IDENTIFIER to mob/.env so StripeProvider can use your Apple merchant ID.",
  3783	        );
  3784	        return;
  3785	      }
  3786	
  3787	      if (!isApplePaySupported) {
  3788	        showPaymentAlert(
  3789	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  3790	          "This iPhone is not ready for Apple Pay. Make sure Wallet has an active card, then try again.",
  3791	        );
  3792	        return;
  3793	      }
  3794	    } else if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod) {
  3795	      if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  3796	        showPaymentAlert(
  3797	          "Google Pay unavailable",
  3798	          "Google Pay is only available on Android. Use Debit/Credit Card on this device.",
  3799	        );
  3800	        return;
  3801	      }
  3802	
  3803	      if (isExpoGo) {
  3804	        showPaymentAlert(
  3805	          "Google Pay needs a development build",
  3806	          "Expo Go cannot open the native Google Pay sheet. Use an Android development build or Play Store build to test Google Pay.",
  3807	        );
  3808	        return;
  3809	      }
  3810	
  3811	      if (!isGooglePaySupported) {
  3812	        showPaymentAlert(
  3813	          "Google Pay unavailable",
  3814	          "This device is not ready for Google Pay. Make sure Google Wallet is set up, then try again.",
  3815	        );
  3816	        return;
  3817	      }
  3818	    } else if (
  3819	      !isPaymentCardAccepted ||
  3820	      !stripeCardDetails?.complete ||
  3821	      !acceptedStripePaymentMethodId
  3822	    ) {
  3823	      showPaymentAlert(
  3824	        "Card details needed",
  3825	        "Enter a complete card number, expiration date, and CVV.",
  3826	      );
  3827	      return;
  3828	    }
  3829	
  3830	    const orderPayload = buildStripeOrderPayload();
  3831	
  3832	    if (orderPayload.items.length === 0) {
  3833	      showPaymentAlert("Cart is empty", "Add an item before placing an order.");
  3834	      return;
  3835	    }
  3836	
  3837	    setIsStripePaymentInFlight(true);
  3838	
  3839	    try {
  3840	      const paymentSheet = await createStripePaymentSheet(orderPayload);
  3841	      if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayApplePayMethod) {
  3842	        await confirmApplePayPayment(
  3843	          paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret,
  3844	          paymentSheet,
  3845	        );
  3846	      } else if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod) {
  3847	        await confirmGooglePayPayment(paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret);
  3848	      } else {
  3849	        const confirmResult = await confirmPayment(
  3850	          paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret,
  3851	          {
  3852	            paymentMethodType: "Card",
  3853	            paymentMethodData: {
  3854	              billingDetails: buildStripeBillingDetails(),
  3855	              paymentMethodId: acceptedStripePaymentMethodId,
  3856	            },
  3857	          },
  3858	        );
  3859	
  3860	        if (confirmResult.error) {
  3861	          throw new Error(
  3862	            confirmResult.error.localizedMessage ||
  3863	              confirmResult.error.message ||
  3864	              "Payment was not completed.",
  3865	          );
  3866	        }
  3867	
  3868	        if (
  3869	          confirmResult.paymentIntent?.status &&
  3870	          !["Succeeded", "Processing"].includes(
  3871	            confirmResult.paymentIntent.status,
  3872	          )
  3873	        ) {
  3874	          throw new Error(
  3875	            `Payment status is ${confirmResult.paymentIntent.status}. Please try again.`,
  3876	          );
  3877	        }
  3878	      }
  3879	
  3880	      showOrderPlacementConfirmation();
  3881	    } catch (error) {
  3882	      showPaymentAlert(
  3883	        "Payment not completed",
  3884	        error?.message || "Please try again.",
  3885	      );
  3886	    } finally {
  3887	      setIsStripePaymentInFlight(false);
  3888	    }
  3889	  };
  3890	  const handleShippingPreviewActionPress = () => {
  3891	    if (isCartAddItemsActionVisible) {
  3892	      showProductOverlayFromCart();
  3893	      return;
  3894	    }
  3895	
  3896	    if (isContactTimeActionVisible) {
  3897	      showTimeOverlayFromContact();
  3898	      return;
  3899	    }
  3900	
  3901	    if (isTimeDeliveryActionVisible) {
  3902	      showDeliveryOverlayFromTime();
  3903	      return;
  3904	    }
  3905	
  3906	    if (isDeliveryPaymentActionVisible) {
  3907	      showPaymentOverlayFromDelivery();
  3908	      return;
  3909	    }
  3910	
  3911	    if (isPaymentViewCartActionVisible) {
  3912	      showCartOverlayFromPayment();
  3913	      return;
  3914	    }
  3915	
  3916	    if (isPlaceholderActionVisible) {
  3917	      showPaymentOverlayFromPlaceholder();
  3918	      return;
  3919	    }
  3920	
  3921	    toggleTruckOverlay();
  3922	  };
  3923	  const handleShippingPreviewLeftActionPress = () => {
  3924	    if (isCartAddItemsActionVisible) {
  3925	      showProductOverlayFromCart();
  3926	      return;
  3927	    }
  3928	
  3929	    if (isContactTimeActionVisible) {
  3930	      showCartOverlayFromContact();
  3931	      return;
  3932	    }
  3933	
  3934	    if (isTimeDeliveryActionVisible) {
  3935	      showContactOverlayFromTime();
  3936	      return;
  3937	    }
  3938	
  3939	    if (isDeliveryPaymentActionVisible) {
  3940	      showTimeOverlayFromDelivery();
  3941	      return;
  3942	    }
  3943	
  3944	    if (isPaymentViewCartActionVisible) {
  3945	      showDeliveryOverlayFromPayment();
  3946	      return;
  3947	    }
  3948	
  3949	    if (isPlaceholderActionVisible) {
  3950	      showPaymentOverlayFromPlaceholder();
  3951	      return;
  3952	    }
  3953	
  3954	    handleShippingPreviewActionPress();
  3955	  };
  3956	  const handleShippingPreviewRightActionPress = () => {
  3957	    if (isProductsActionVisible) {
  3958	      showCartOverlayFromProducts();
  3959	      return;
  3960	    }
  3961	
  3962	    if (isCartAddItemsActionVisible) {
  3963	      showContactOverlayFromCart();
  3964	      return;
  3965	    }
  3966	
  3967	    if (isContactTimeActionVisible) {
  3968	      showTimeOverlayFromContact();
  3969	      return;
  3970	    }

  5194	            {rowFields.map((field) => {

  5196	                return renderOverlayFieldSpacer(field);
  5197	              }

    96	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    97	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
    98	  if (pathname === "/aboutus") return "aboutus";
    99	  if (pathname === "/contact") return "contact";
   100	  if (pathname === "/shop") return "shop";
   101	  return "home";
   102	}
   103	
   104	function getNavigationDirection(fromPage, toPage) {
   105	  const fromIndex = navPages.indexOf(fromPage);
   106	  const toIndex = navPages.indexOf(toPage);
   107	
   108	  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) return 1;
   109	
   110	  const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   111	
   112	  if (fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0) return 1;
   113	  if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex) return -1;
   114	
   115	  return toIndex > fromIndex ? 1 : -1;
   116	}
